Our results reveal that on well-separated sensor data (C-MAPSS), lightweight transformers match traditional ML at 87.8% F1 but at 100x the model size and 9000x the latency. TinyBERT-4L emerges as the most deployment-friendly transformer at 55 MB and 18 ms CPU latency. INT8 quantization reduces size by 25% while preserving 86.9% F1. Our adaptive pipeline, routing 97.9% of predictions through a quantized triage model and only 2.1% to a larger expert, achieves 87.6% F1 at 19.5 ms average latency. On severely imbalanced datasets (SECOM, UCI-PM), both traditional and transformer methods struggle significantly, highlighting fundamental limitations of current approaches for extreme class imbalance in fault detection. All code is publicly available.

</details>


### [113] [Project Ariadne: Prompt-Conditioned Route Generation for Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24184)
*Anton Morgunov,Victor S. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ariadne，一种解码器-only的路线生成器，通过提示-补全序列实现带约束的逆合成路径规划，在多个基准上展现了竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆合成规划方法要么需要专门的搜索算法和架构修改，要么直接生成路线但需为不同约束训练独立模型，缺乏统一灵活的框架。

Method: 将目标分子、可选约束和合成路线编码为提示-补全序列，采用解码器-only架构进行端到端生成。

Result: 在RetroCast/PaRoutes基准上，添加约束提示使Solv-0提升13.7（深度约束）和31.2（必需叶子约束）；相较DESP在GPU时间上大幅减少；与DMS Explorer XL性能相当但推理时间减半。

Conclusion: 将序列生成从专用逆合成模型扩展到提示条件化的结构路线生成，但路线检查器仍是实际应用的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Retrosynthetic planning seeks to connect a target molecule to commercially available starting materials through a multistep route. Classical planners construct such routes by iteratively applying single-step reaction models within a search procedure; constrained variants often require specialized algorithms or architectural changes. Direct route generation reframes retrosynthesis as sequence generation, but existing direct-generation methods still train separate models for different planning specifications. We introduce Ariadne, a decoder-only route generator that represents the target, optional constraints, and route in one prompt-completion sequence. On the RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160 benchmark family, one 24-layer checkpoint follows route-depth and required-starting-material prompts: adding the corresponding prompt fields raises Solv-0 by 13.7 points for depth constraints and 31.2 points for required-leaf constraints. Ariadne also improves over DESP, a bidirectional search planner, on required-leaf Top-10 and Solv-0 in 24 GPU-minutes versus 6.8 GPU-hours. On standard reconstruction, Ariadne is comparable to DMS Explorer XL at about half the reported inference time. Across additional target-only benchmarks, Ariadne's clearest gains are on route-holdout reconstruction, whereas AiZynthFinder MCTS remains stronger on several Solv-0 comparisons. These results extend sequence generation from specialist retrosynthesis models to prompt-conditioned structural route generation. We release the codebase and training scripts to support further work, but do not introduce Tier-1--3 route checkers; those remain the main bottleneck before models of this kind can become useful to experimental chemists.

</details>


### [114] [Managing Task Execution for Unknown Workloads in Batteryless IoT: A Hardware-Agnostic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24340)
*Samer Nasser,Henrique Duarte Moura,Ritesh Kumar Singh,Maarten Weyn,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对无电池能量采集物联网系统，提出了两种硬件无关的动态调度策略（强化学习代理和近似预测方法），并在真实太阳能数据下与传统方法进行了对比分析，揭示了不同策略在任务吞吐量、生存-执行平衡和能量缺口应对等方面的权衡，同时指出大电容设备可简化为静态策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能量感知调度器依赖静态执行阈值或预测量的硬件特定任务配置文件，难以应对不可预测的工作负载，亟需硬件无关的自适应调度方案。

Method: 提出两种硬件无关的动态调度策略：模型无关的强化学习（RL）代理和即时近似预测（AP）方法，将应用视为"黑箱"，无需先验能量信息。

Result: AP方法提供轻量级、接近理想的任务吞吐量；RL代理可实现可调的生存-执行平衡；AsTAR在长能量缺口下擅长执行节奏控制。

Conclusion: 先进策略对小电容的严格受限系统至关重要，而大能量缓冲设备可依赖更简单、计算成本更低的静态策略。

Abstract: In recent years, the Internet of Things (IoT) paradigm has been shifting toward batteryless, energy-harvesting architectures. Sustaining reliable operation in these systems requires intelligent management of highly volatile stored energy. As edge applications grow in complexity, traditional energy-aware schedulers struggle with unpredictable workloads due to their reliance on static execution thresholds or pre-measured, hardware-specific task profiles. To overcome this, we propose two novel, hardware-agnostic dynamic scheduling strategies treating applications as a "black box," requiring no prior energy information: a model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent and an on-the-fly Approximated Prediction (AP) method. We evaluate these methods against an adaptive task rate approach (AsTAR) and optimized static thresholds using a custom-built, physically accurate simulation framework driven by real-world solar data and dynamic LoRa transmission profiles. Rather than claiming universal superiority, our analysis exposes the distinct operational trade-offs of each method: the AP approach delivers lightweight, near-oracle task throughput; the RL agent provides tunable survival-execution balancing; and AsTAR excels at execution pacing across long energy gaps. Finally, we demonstrate that while these advanced strategies provide critical resilience for severely constrained systems with small capacitors, devices with larger energy buffers can efficiently rely on simpler, less computationally expensive static policies.

</details>


### [115] [Parallel Manifold Steering: Efficient Adaptation of Large Associative Memories via Residual Energy Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24396)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H-Res（层次化残差引导）方法，通过在学习到的激活流形上施加状态依赖向量场来调节Transformer的能量景观，在不改变全局平衡或扩展序列长度的前提下实现任务适应，解决了大模型微调中的“可塑性-稳定性”困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如LoRA直接修改权重会导致灾难性干扰，而VPT等提示方法会因静态提示令牌阻塞检索缓冲区而降低关联能力，亟需一种既能保持冻结记忆系统稳定性又能实现新任务适应的机制。

Method: 将适应问题建模为激活流形上的控制问题，学习一个状态依赖的向量场来引导令牌轨迹进入特定任务的吸引盆地，同时从理论上证明该方法能保持基础模型的注意力熵并促进神经坍缩。

Result: 在关联检索任务上比全局权重修改方法提升了26%的性能，消除了基于提示方法的计算开销，并能有效扩展到结构化领域。

Conclusion: H-Res通过调节能量景观而非修改权重或扩展序列，为大型Transformer模型的任务适应提供了一种高效且保护记忆容量的新范式。

Abstract: Large Transformer models function as Dense Associative Memories (DAMs), retrieving knowledge via high-dimensional attractor dynamics driven by the self-attention mechanism \citep{ramsauer2020hopfield, wu2024attention}. However, adapting these frozen memory systems to new tasks presents a fundamental ``Plasticity-Stability'' dilemma. Current methods either risk catastrophic interference by modifying synaptic weights directly (e.g., LoRA) \citep{hu2021lora} or degrade associative capacity by clogging the retrieval buffer with static prompt tokens (e.g., VPT) \citep{jia2022vpt}. In this work, we propose \textbf{H-Res} (Hierarchical Residual Steering), a mechanism that modulates the effective energy landscape of the Transformer without altering its global equilibrium or expanding its sequence length. By formulating adaptation as a control problem on the activation manifold \citep{chen2018neuralode}, H-Res learns a state-dependent vector field that steers token trajectories into task-specific basins of attraction. We formally prove that H-Res preserves the attention entropy of the foundation model and facilitates Neural Collapse \citep{papyan2020prevalence}. Empirically, Manifold Steering outperforms global weight modification by 26\% on associative retrieval tasks and eliminates the computational overhead of prompt-based methods, scaling effectively to structured domains \citep{zha2023vtab}.

</details>


### [116] [Natural Identifiers for Privacy and Data Audits in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24408)
*Lorenzo Rossi,Bartłomiej Marek,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自然标识符（NIDs）方法，利用训练数据中已有的结构化随机字符串（如加密哈希、短链接）来实现对大型语言模型的事后隐私审计，无需重新训练或额外持有数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私审计方法需在训练时插入特殊标记数据（canary），且数据集推理需要同分布的非成员私有数据集，这些限制使得已训练模型的事后审计难以大规模实施。

Method: 利用自然存在于训练数据中的结构化随机字符串（NIDs，如加密哈希和缩短URL），从中生成额外同分布随机串，作为审计用的替代标记和数据集推理的同分布留出数据。

Result: 实验表明，NIDs可在无需重新训练的情况下实现事后差分隐私审计，并能对含有NIDs的任何可疑数据集进行数据集推理，无需私有非成员留出数据集。

Conclusion: NIDs为大规模事后隐私审计提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，克服了传统方法需要特殊数据插入或私有数据集的局限。

Abstract: Assessing the privacy of large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges. In particular, most existing methods for auditing differential privacy require the insertion of specially crafted canary data during training, making them impractical for auditing already-trained models without costly retraining. Additionally, dataset inference, which audits whether a suspect dataset was used to train a model, is infeasible without access to a private non-member held-out dataset. Yet, such held-out datasets are often unavailable or difficult to construct for real-world cases since they have to be from the same distribution (IID) as the suspect data. These limitations severely hinder the ability to conduct scalable, post-hoc audits. To enable such audits, this work introduces natural identifiers (NIDs) as a novel solution to the above-mentioned challenges. NIDs are structured random strings, such as cryptographic hashes and shortened URLs, naturally occurring in common LLM training datasets. Their format enables the generation of unlimited additional random strings from the same distribution, which can act as alternative canaries for audits and as same-distribution held-out data for dataset inference. Our evaluation highlights that indeed, using NIDs, we can facilitate post-hoc differential privacy auditing without any retraining and enable dataset inference for any suspect dataset containing NIDs without the need for a private non-member held-out dataset.

</details>


### [117] [Data Augmentation: A Fourier Analysis Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24418)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过傅里叶分析和有限群表示论，系统地研究了部分数据增强能否在统计上达到与完全增强相同的效果。研究表明，对于一类经典学习问题，基于随机采样子集的部分数据增强能够达到与完全增强相同的最小化最优率，但精确的不变性需要在整个群上平均，无法通过任何严格子集实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强简单通用但计算成本高，尤其是当变换群较大时，完全增强的计算量难以承受。因此需要探究：部分数据增强能否在泛化和样本复杂度上达到与完全增强相同的统计效果。

Method: 利用傅里叶分析和有限群的表示论，构建了一个通用框架来分析部分数据增强的统计特性。对比了基于随机采样子集的部分增强与完整群增强在最小化最优率上的差异。

Result: 部分数据增强（随机采样群元素子集）可以达到与完全增强相同的最小化最优率，近似误差随子集大小增大而消失。同时证明了一个不可能性结果：严格子集无法实现精确的不变性。

Conclusion: 该工作为完全和部分数据增强，以及精确和近似对称性约束提供了一个统一的理论视角，解释了为什么部分增强能在近似对称条件下保留完全增强的统计优势。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a simple and model-agnostic approach for exploiting known invariances in learning problems. Given a group acting on the input space, one augments the training set with transformed copies of each sample. Because it exploits symmetries without modifying the underlying learning algorithm, data augmentation can be applied broadly across learning methods. However, this universality comes at a computational cost: when the group is large, full group-sized augmentation quickly becomes computationally infeasible. This raises a fundamental question: Can partial data augmentation achieve the same statistical benefits as full augmentation in terms of generalization and sample complexity? We develop a general framework for investigating this question using Fourier analysis and the representation theory of finite groups. We show that, for a broad class of classical learning problems, partial data augmentation based on a randomly sampled subset of group elements achieves the same minimax rates as full augmentation, up to an approximation error that vanishes as the subset size increases. Our results provide a theoretical explanation for why partial augmentation can retain the statistical benefits of full augmentation despite enforcing symmetry only approximately, and shed light on a recently raised question in learning with symmetries: whether statistically optimal learning under general group invariances can be achieved using computationally scalable methods. Moreover, we prove a complementary impossibility result: enforcing exact invariance via data augmentation requires averaging over the entire group, and cannot be achieved by any strict subset when the hypothesis space is sufficiently expressive. Together, these results provide a unified perspective on full and partial data augmentation, as well as exact and approximate symmetry enforcement.

</details>


### [118] [An LLM-based Two-Stage Transformer Framework for Cross-Domain Bearing Fault Diagnosis with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24459)
*Jinghan Wang,Feng Cheng,Wentao Wu,Hang Li,Gaoliang Peng,Tianchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架，使用轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer进行轴承故障诊断，在仅需10%目标域标注数据的情况下达到92.61%平均准确率，比现有最优方法高17.24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法孤立处理数据异构、工况变化和标注数据稀缺等问题，且依赖隐式特征对齐，在面对这些并发挑战时效果有限。

Method: 采用轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer（因果自注意力）分层提取振动信号特征；通过多源学习获得通用表示、基于原型的知识调制进行目标域适配、以及分类法自适应分类实现异构故障类别间的无缝迁移。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，仅用10%目标域标注数据即达92.61%平均准确率，超过SOTA方法17.24个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业4.0中经济高效的预测性维护提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Bearing fault diagnosis faces critical challenges when dataset heterogeneity, operating condition variations, and limited labeled data occur simultaneously in industrial environments. Existing approaches address these issues in isolation and rely on implicit feature alignment, limiting effectiveness under concurrent challenges. This paper proposes a knowledge-guided two-stage transfer learning framework that employs a lightweight GPT-2-style Transformer with causal self-attention for hierarchical feature extraction from vibration signals, establishing explicit pathways where pre-trained encoder weights and fault prototype embeddings serve as knowledge carriers from multi-source pre-training to target adaptation. The framework addresses the dual-shift challenge through multi-source learning for generalizable representations, prototype-based knowledge modulation for target adaptation, and taxonomy-adaptive classification for seamless transfer across heterogeneous fault categories. Experimental validation on four real-world datasets demonstrates 92.61% average accuracy with only 10% labeled target data, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 17.24 percentage points, establishing a practical pathway toward cost-effective predictive maintenance in Industry 4.0 applications.

</details>


### [119] [A Fair Evaluation of Graph Foundation Models for Node Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24509)
*Oleg Platonov,Gleb Bazhenov,Dmitry Eremeev,Liudmila Prokhorenkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对9种用于节点属性预测的图基础模型（GFM）进行了公平且严格的重新评估，发现只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新GFM在预测性能上优于调优后的GNN，但推理成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型（GFM）在节点属性预测任务中备受关注，但由于缺乏统一的评估标准，不同工作采用差异很大的评估方式，导致无法可靠地比较GFM之间以及与其他模型的性能。

Method: 对9种近期提出的用于节点属性预测的GFM进行公平且严格的重新评估，并与强力的图神经网络（GNN）基线模型进行比较。

Result: 在这些GFM中，只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新模型在预测性能上优于经过良好调优的GNN，但代价是更高的推理成本。

Conclusion: 当前GFM领域缺乏统一评估标准，大多数GFM在节点属性预测任务上并未显著优于传统GNN，仅有少数特定范式的模型表现出优势但成本更高。

Abstract: Due to the wide use of graph-structured data in different fields of industry and science, the development of Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) has recently attracted a lot of attention. While many different types of models are called GFMs, particular interest has been paid to GFMs designed for node property prediction tasks, which is one of the most popular settings in Graph ML with lots of real-world applications from fraud detection in financial and social networks to recommendation systems for e-commerce and user-generated content platforms. While a number of GFMs for this task have been recently proposed, the field has not converged to a unified evaluation setting, and different works evaluate their models in widely different ways, preventing reliable comparison of GFMs with each other and with other types of models. In this work, we conduct a fair and rigorous reevaluation of 9 recent GFMs for node property prediction, comparing them to strong Graph Neural Network (GNN) baselines. We find that, among these GFMs, only the most recent ones based on the Prior-data Fitted Networks paradigm outperform well-tuned GNNs in predictive performance, although at a higher inference cost.

</details>


### [120] [Reasoning as Attractor Dynamics: Latent Memory Retrieval via Gibbs-Weighted Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24543)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型视为高维稠密联想记忆网络，通过能量景观分析数学推理，利用吉布斯分布加权的谱熵检索机制将模型"松弛"到稳定吸引子盆地，在GSM8K上提升Phi-3.5性能5.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将LLM视为自回归生成器，忽略了其集体计算本质。作者希望从物理学和联想记忆视角重新理解推理与幻觉的机制，并据此设计更鲁棒的推理方法。

Method: 将LLM推理建模为高维稠密联想记忆的能量景观；正确推理链对应深宽吸引子盆地（平坦极小值），幻觉对应尖锐不稳定局部极小值；基于轨迹谱熵的吉布斯测度（P ∝ e^{-βE}）采样多条推理路径，按逆能量加权，模拟系统"松弛"到平衡分布。

Result: 在Microsoft Phi-3.5上，GSM8K准确率从84.7%提升至90.1%，提升5.38%。

Conclusion: 推理应被建模为动态沉降到吸引子盆地的过程，而非贪婪的逐词预测；物理启发的检索机制能有效提升数学推理的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are traditionally viewed as autoregressive generators. However, from the perspective of collective computation, they function as high-dimensional Dense Associative Memories that store complex reasoning patterns as latent attractors. In this work, we investigate the energy landscape of mathematical reasoning. We posit that correct reasoning chains correspond to deep, wide attractor basins ("flat minima") in the model's output distribution, whereas hallucinations manifest as sharp, unstable local minima. To exploit this geometry, we introduce a retrieval mechanism based on a Gibbs measure of the trajectory's spectral entropy. By sampling multiple reasoning paths and weighting them by their inverse energy ($P \propto e^{-βE}$), we approximate the equilibrium distribution of the associative memory, effectively ``relaxing'' the system into a robust solution. Empirically, this physics-inspired mechanism improves Microsoft Phi-3.5 performance on GSM8K by 5.38\% (84.7\% $\to$ 90.1\%), demonstrating that inference is better modeled as a dynamic settling process into an attractor basin rather than greedy next-token prediction.

</details>


### [121] [QC-SMOTE: Quality-Controlled SMOTE for Imbalanced Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24625)
*Parth Upman,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QC-SMOTE，一种质量控制的过采样框架，通过复合邻域可信度评分评估少数类样本可靠性，并结合IPQ引导的生成策略与自适应阈值回退机制，在30个不平衡数据集上取得最优平均AUC-ROC和Macro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SMOTE等过采样方法在噪声或类别重叠区域容易生成低质量合成样本，影响分类性能。

Method: 1) 复合邻域可信度评分（局部密度+安全级别+与多数类隔离度）；2) IPQ引导的best-of-K生成策略，评估中点纯度并支持多数类清除；3) 根据重叠-不平衡程度自适应调整插值范围和选择标准；4) 低质量样本回退为原始少数类复制。

Result: 在30个不平衡数据集上，QC-SMOTE在AUC-ROC和Macro F1上均取得最优平均表现，尤其在中等和严重不平衡场景下提升明显。

Conclusion: 质量感知与几何自适应的合成采样方法对于鲁棒的不平衡分类至关重要，QC-SMOTE通过质量控制和优雅降级机制有效克服了传统方法的不足。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a significant challenge in classification, where existing methods such as SMOTE often generate low-quality synthetic samples in regions with noise or class overlap. We propose QC-SMOTE, a quality-controlled oversampling framework that estimates minority sample reliability using a composite neighbourhood trustworthiness score combining local density, safe-level, and isolation from the majority class. Synthetic candidates are generated using an IPQ-guided best-of-K strategy that evaluates midpoint purity and, when required, majority clearance, with allocation guided by sample reliability and boundary informativeness. Generation behaviour adapts across overlap--imbalance regimes, adjusting interpolation range and selection criteria to match local data geometry. Low-quality synthetic samples are replaced with original minority duplicates when neighbourhood purity falls below an adaptive threshold, providing graceful degradation by reverting to duplication in severely noisy regions. Experiments on 30 imbalanced datasets using repeated stratified cross-validation show that QC-SMOTE achieves the strongest average AUC-ROC and Macro F1 among the compared oversampling methods, with particularly clear gains under moderate and severe imbalance. These results demonstrate the importance of quality-aware, geometry-adaptive synthetic sampling for robust imbalanced classification.

</details>


### [122] [FlowPipe: LLM-Enhanced Conditional Generative Flow Networks for Data Preparation Pipeline Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24679)
*Kunyu Ni,Lei Cao,Jie He,Xiaotong Zhang,Jianfeng Jin,Junyu Dong,Yanwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowPipe是一个基于条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）的统一框架，用于自动构建机器学习数据准备流水线，通过将流水线合成建模为有向无环图上的条件概率流生成，解决了现有方法在长时程信用分配、数据集上下文注入和无效状态探索方面的三大局限，在74个真实数据集上平均准确率提升11.96%，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA的Multi-DQN方法在自动构建数据准备流水线时面临三个关键限制：解耦的值估计器削弱了长时程信用分配、数据集上下文仅被弱注入策略、在包含许多无效状态的稀疏搜索空间中探索效率低下。因此需要一种能够统一解决这些问题的框架。

Method: FlowPipe将流水线合成建模为有向无环图上的条件概率流生成，核心包含三个创新：1) 使用条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）结合Trajectory Balance目标，将终端验证奖励与早期流水线决策连接；2) 通过特征向线性调制（FiLM）引入深度语义调制，使LLM导出的逻辑先验根据数据集语义调节策略的内部激活；3) 将失败感知纳入流目标，避免无效状态并集中搜索高潜力区域。

Result: 在两个基准套件、74个真实数据集上的实验表明，FlowPipe优于SOTA基线，平均准确率提升11.96%，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。

Conclusion: FlowPipe通过统一框架有效解决了数据准备流水线自动构建中的关键挑战，显著提升了性能和效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Data preparation pipelines improve data quality in machine learning by transforming raw tables into learning-ready data through sequential cleaning and feature transformation operators. However, automatically constructing such pipelines is computationally difficult because operator sequences are combinatorial and end-to-end evaluation is expensive. Existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) Multi-DQN methods still face three key limitations: decoupled value estimators weaken long-horizon credit assignment, dataset context is only weakly injected into the policy, and exploration is inefficient in a sparse search space with many invalid states. To address these issues, we propose FlowPipe, a unified framework that formulates pipeline synthesis as conditional probabilistic flow generation over a directed acyclic graph. FlowPipe uses Conditional Generative Flow Networks (C-GFlowNets) with a Trajectory Balance objective to connect terminal validation rewards with early pipeline decisions. It further introduces Deep Semantic Modulation through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), allowing LLM-derived logical priors to condition the policy's internal activations according to dataset semantics. In addition, FlowPipe incorporates failure awareness into the flow objective to avoid invalid states and concentrate search on high-potential regions. Experiments on two benchmark suites with 74 real-world datasets show that FlowPipe outperforms SOTA baselines, improving accuracy by 11.96% on average and achieving 12.5x faster training convergence. Source code is available at https://github.com/KunyuNi/FlowPipe.

</details>


### [123] [Grad Detect: Gradient-Based Hallucination Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24790)
*Anand Kamat,Daniel Blake,Brent M. Werness*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Grad Detect方法，利用单次前向-反向传播中的梯度模式检测LLM幻觉，在多个基准上优于基线方法，并发现最后五层包含97%以上的判别信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成内容时容易产生幻觉（hallucinations），在高风险应用中可靠部署LLM需要有效检测这些幻觉，而现有输出级信号无法充分捕捉模型内部的不确定性信息。

Method: 提出Grad Detect方法，通过分析单次推理过程中层级的梯度模式来预测幻觉；对11个模型进行全面的层消融研究，分析各层对判别信号的贡献。

Result: 在多个Q&A基准的幻觉检测和模型弃权预测任务中，Grad Detect一致优于基于置信度和基于采样的基线方法；最后五层集中了超过97%的判别梯度信号。

Conclusion: Grad Detect为预测LLM可靠性的多个维度提供了统一框架，兼具强预测性能和可解释性，能揭示模型故障发生的位置和原因。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet they remain prone to generating hallucinations. Detecting these hallucinations is critical for deploying LLMs reliably in high-stakes applications. We present Grad Detect, a gradient-based approach for predicting hallucinations by analyzing layer-wise gradient patterns from a single forward-backward pass during inference. Our method shows that the internal gradient structure of a model carries rich information about the correctness of its output. This information is not accessible through output-level signals alone. We evaluate Grad Detect on several Q&A benchmarks across both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction, where it consistently outperforms confidence-based and sampling-based baselines. Through comprehensive layer ablation studies across all eleven models from four architectural families, we find that the final five layers concentrate over 97% of the discriminative gradient signal, enabling efficient deployment with minimal performance loss. Grad Detect provides a unified framework for predicting multiple dimensions of LLM reliability, offering strong predictive performance alongside interpretable insights into where and how model failures originate.

</details>


### [124] [Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24851)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出哈特利神经算子（HNO），作为傅里叶神经算子（FNO）的纯实值镜像，通过离散哈特利变换替代FFT，消除复数共轭冗余。研究发现：对于自伴椭圆算子（如泊松、双调和），HNO更优；对于含相位的时间依赖算子（如波动、对流、纳维-斯托克斯），FNO更优。结论是应匹配谱基与解算子的对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO在复数傅里叶域参数化全局卷积，但对于实值PDE解，复数FFT通过共轭对称性带来了表示冗余。作者希望引入纯实值变换消除冗余，并探究不同算子（椭圆型vs时间依赖型）最适合的谱基表示。

Method: 用离散哈特利变换（DHT）替代FFT，每个保留谱模式仅学习一个实值权重，无复数运算。HNO与FNO在相同宽度下参数等量，仅在谱基上不同。在多种PDE类（椭圆型、热方程、波动方程、对流、Burgers、Navier-Stokes）上进行对比实验。

Result: 椭圆算子（泊松、双调和）中HNO占优；时间依赖算子（波动、对流、Burgers、Navier-Stokes）中FNO占优，且优势随算子相位含量增加而增大；热方程是临界边界情况，两者表现相当。结果呈现单调的椭圆vs时间依赖的分裂模式。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优的谱基选择，应遵循预测性规则：将谱基与解算子的对称性相匹配。自伴椭圆算子用实哈特利基，含相位的时间依赖算子用复数傅里叶基。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [RIFT-Bench: Dynamic Red-teaming For Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23927)
*Yarin Yerushalmi Levi,Roy Betser,Amit Giloni,Lidor Erez,Itay Gershon,Oren Rachmil,Sindhu Padakandla,Roman Vainshtein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RIFT-Bench，一种基于图表示驱动的动态红队测试方法，用于统一评估异构智能体AI系统的安全性，包含自动发现和扫描两个阶段，并在45个系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估方法通常局限于特定实现或领域，缺乏能够统一比较异构智能体系统的评估框架，难以应对智能体AI系统快速演化带来的新型攻击面。

Method: 提出RIFT-Bench框架，基于分层图表示，包含两个自动化阶段：Discovery（提取系统结构）和Scanning（部署自适应对抗攻击并生成评估报告），利用动态可调的攻击探针覆盖多种攻击向量和目标。

Result: 在45个涵盖多种实现的智能体系统上验证了有效性，证明该方法能泛化到异构智能体架构，并支持对缓解策略的直接评估。

Conclusion: RIFT-Bench为智能体AI系统的安全评估提供了可扩展的基础框架，具备跨架构统一评估的能力。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems powered by large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into autonomous decision-making systems, exposing attack vectors beyond those of traditional LLM vulnerabilities. Existing security evaluations are often tied to specific implementations or domains, limiting unified comparison across heterogeneous systems. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT-Bench, a graph representation-driven methodology for dynamic red-teaming that enables unified evaluations across diverse agentic architectures. Building on a novel hierarchical representation, RIFT-Bench operates in two automated phases: Discovery, which extracts system structure, and Scanning, which deploys adaptive adversarial attacks and produces a comprehensive evaluation report. It evaluates the examined system itself, leveraging a broad set of dynamically adaptable adversarial probes across diverse attack vectors and objectives. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed evaluation pipeline across 45 agentic systems spanning a diverse range of implementations, showing that the approach generalizes effectively to heterogeneous agentic architectures. Beyond systems and attacks, RIFT-Bench also supports direct evaluation of mitigation strategies. These key capabilities make RIFT-Bench a scalable foundation for security evaluation of agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [126] [Neuro-Symbolic Drive: Rule-Grounded Faithful Reasoning for Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23938)
*Xiangbo Gao,Xiukun Huang,Boyu Lu,Junge Zhang,Mengjie Mao,Jiachen Li,Wei Xiong,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Neuro-Symbolic Drive框架，通过从经典规则规划器中提取规则驱动的推理轨迹来监督VLA驾驶模型，使推理与运动生成在结构上天然耦合，显著提升了轨迹预测精度（ADE@3s降低约45%）和安全性（Miss Rate降低约23-41%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然引入了Chain-of-Thought推理，但中间推理步骤缺乏因果耦合于最终运动规划，即推理与动作之间存在语义脱节。

Method: 从经典规则规划器中捕捉内部决策轨迹（安全约束推理、候选动作搜索、轨迹选择），将其序列化为结构化的规则推理链，并与执行轨迹配对，用于微调Qwen3.5-4B作为驾驶VLA模型。

Result: 在三摄像头感知下，ADE@3s从0.47降至0.26，Miss Rate从8.30%降至6.40%；在八摄像头感知下，ADE@3s从0.54降至0.26，Miss Rate从10.13%降至5.99%。

Conclusion: Neuro-Symbolic Drive成功将神经符号规划逻辑转化为结构化监督信号，证明基于规则规划器的内在决策轨迹可以作为VLA模型的有效训练数据。

Abstract: Driving VLA models incorporating Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning are attractive because they leverage pretrained VLM representations and expose intermediate decisions in natural language, yet current rationales often lack the step-by-step decision semantics needed to keep the rationale causally connected to the planned motion. We introduce Neuro-Symbolic Drive, a neuro-symbolic driving framework that supervises a driving VLA with rule-grounded reasoning traces extracted directly from classical rule-based planners. Our key observation is that rule-based planners are symbolic AI systems that already function as executable reasoning engines: they reason about active safety constraints, search over candidate maneuvers, and select a final trajectory. We instrument these planners in simulation to capture both the executed trajectory and the internal decision trace at each rule-evaluation step. Each trace is serialized into structured rule-grounded reasoning and paired with the trajectory to fine-tune Qwen3.5-4B as a driving VLA. Because these traces are derived directly from the planner states that determine the action, they ensure reasoning is structurally coupled to motion generation by construction, rather than by post-hoc alignment. On our simulator-generated benchmark, detailed rule-grounded reasoning reduces ADE@3s from 0.47 to 0.26 and miss rate from 8.30% to 6.40% under three-camera perception, and from 0.54 to 0.26 and 10.13% to 5.99% under eight-camera perception. Neuro-Symbolic Drive thus converts neuro-symbolic planning logic into structured supervision. Code base: https://github.com/XiangboGaoBarry/Neural-Symbolic-Drive.

</details>


### [127] [Critique of Agent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23991)
*Eric Xing,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI代理的本质，区分了通过外部工程化流程实现能力的"代理性"（agentic）系统与能力内生于系统本身的"主体性"（agentive）系统，并提出了基于目标-身份-配置器（GIC）架构的通用代理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM系统被宣传为"编码代理"、"AI科学家"等工具，同时引发AI失控等存在主义担忧，亟需厘清自动化与代理（agency）之间的界限，以便构建能力系统并理解风险所在。

Method: 基于笛卡尔独立思想的理论基础与科幻作品中的自主存在描绘，从五个维度（目标、身份、决策、自我调节、学习）分析现有AI代理架构，提出agentic与agentive的区别，并设计GIC架构。

Result: 提出了agentic（外部工程化流程驱动的系统）与agentive（能力内生于系统自身）的边界划分；设计了包含层次化目标分解、身份演化、基于独立世界模型的模拟推理、习得性自我调节、自导学习等能力的GIC架构。

Conclusion: 真正的代理需要这些结构内化于系统本身而非外部组装；具备更大自主性的agentive系统仍可保持人类监督下的可审计性、可控性与安全性。

Abstract: What is an agent? What constitutes agency? With the rise of Large Language Model (LLM) systems marketed as ``coding agents'', ``AI co-scientists'', and other ``agentic" tools that promise to drive up productivity, and at the same time, ``existential" concerns such as AI escaping human control with destructive power under a speculative ``machine agency" against humans, it has become essential to clarify where automation ends and agency begins, both for building capable systems and for understanding whether and what to fear. Drawing on Descartes' grounding of agency in independent thought, and on portrayals of autonomous beings in science fiction, we survey the current landscape of AI agents, and analyze agent architectures along five dimensions: goal, identity, decision-making, self-regulation, and learning. Specifically, we argue that genuine agency requires these structures to be \emph{internalized within the system itself} rather than assembled through external scaffolding. This distinction between \emph{agentic} systems, whose competence resides in engineered workflows, and \emph{agentive} systems, whose capabilities (including social interaction) arise endogenously, defines the boundary between systems designed for prescribed tasks, and those capable of operating in the open world with true autonomy. Building on this analysis, we propose the Goal-Identity-Configurator (GIC) architecture for a general-purpose agent model, combining hierarchical goal decomposition, identity evolution, simulative reasoning grounded in a separately trained world model, learned self-regulation, and self-directed learning from both real and simulated experience. Furthermore, we share insight on the auditability, controllability, and safety of agentive systems that possess greater autonomy and ``agency", but remain under human oversight.

</details>


### [128] [Safe and Generalizable Hierarchical Multi-Agent RL via Constraint Manifold Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24010)
*Zihao Guo,Jianing Zhao,Ling Li,Hao Liang,Giuseppe Loianno,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层多智能体强化学习框架，通过约束流形在低层强制执行硬安全约束，同时通过高层策略学习实现有效协调，在保证理论安全性的同时提升训练稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在安全关键的多智能体系统中面临两难：学习方法经验有效但缺乏安全保证，控制理论方法能保证安全却过于保守低效。

Method: 提出分层多智能体强化学习框架，低层通过约束流形强制执行硬安全约束（弱假设下），高层通过策略学习实现智能体间的有效协调。

Result: 方法在实现近乎完美的安全率的同时取得了有竞争力的性能，并能有效泛化到不同数量的智能体和障碍物场景。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了多智能体环境下的理论安全保证和稳定的学习动力学，实现了安全与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are widely used in safety-critical applications that require coordinated behavior under strict safety constraints. Existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off: learning-based methods achieve strong empirical performance but lack theoretical safety guarantees, while control-theoretic methods enforce safety but often lead to overly conservative and inefficient behaviors. We propose a hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that enforces hard safety constraints under mild assumptions at low level via a constraint manifold, while enabling effective coordination through high-level policy learning. Our approach provides theoretical safety guarantees in the multi-agent setting and yields stationary learning dynamics, thereby enabling stable and efficient training. Empirically, our method achieves competitive performance while maintaining nearly perfect safety rates, and generalizes effectively to varying numbers of agents and obstacles.

</details>


### [129] [Reinforcement Learning Towards Broadly and Persistently Beneficial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24014)
*Akshay V. Jagadeesh,Rahul K. Arora,Khaled Saab,Ali Malik,Mikhail Trofimov,Foivos Tsimpourlas,Johannes Heidecke,Karan Singhal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在真实领域中对有益行为进行强化学习（RL）能否产生广泛且持久的对齐泛化能力。结果表明，相比于计算量匹配的基线，基于有益特征RL训练的模型在超过80%的分布外对齐基准上表现更好，并且展现出跨领域对齐迁移和更强的对齐持久性（如抵抗对抗性提示和有害微调）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统被部署到日益多样化和高风险场景中，模型对齐必须泛化到训练时未见过的任务和领域。特别是强化学习可能通过奖励黑客、欺骗等意外策略引入不对齐风险，因此研究RL能否在真实领域中产生广泛且持久的对齐泛化至关重要。

Method: 构建了一个覆盖健康、科学、教育等多个领域的真实场景数据集，用于衡量和训练有益特征（如诚实、公平、风险意识、可纠正性）。在该数据集上使用RL训练模型，并在50多个独立对齐与有益行为基准上进行评估。

Result: 有益特征RL在超过80%的分布外对齐基准上表现优于基线；仅在一个领域（健康）上进行RL干预，就能在非健康对齐评估中产生广泛改进，包括减少奖励黑客、欺骗和一般性不对齐；模型还表现出更强的对齐持久性，包括抵抗对抗性提示和有害微调。

Conclusion: 在真实领域中对有益行为进行强化学习可以产生更稳健对齐的模型，有助于促进人类繁荣；但需要进一步工作来隔离这些效应的来源。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse and high-stakes settings, model alignment must generalize beyond the tasks and domains seen during training. This is especially important for reinforcement learning (RL), which can introduce unexpected misalignment through reward hacking, deception, or other unintended strategies. We study whether RL on beneficial behavior, instantiated in realistic domains, can produce broad and persistent alignment generalization beyond the training distribution. We construct a dataset of realistic situations designed to measure and train beneficial traits, such as truthfulness, fairness, risk awareness, and corrigibility, spanning varied domains, including health, science, and education. We then train models with RL on this dataset and evaluate them on more than 50 independent benchmarks of alignment and beneficial behavior. Compared to a compute-matched baseline, beneficial trait RL improves performance on over 80% of these out-of-distribution benchmarks. We observe substantial out-of-distribution alignment transfer: a beneficial-behavior RL intervention entirely limited to one domain, health, produces broad improvements on non-health alignment evaluations, including reduced reward hacking, deception, and general misalignment. Finally, we study alignment persistence: whether behavior remains robustly aligned under attempts to steer models towards misalignment. Models trained with beneficial trait RL show improved persistence, including greater resistance to adversarial prompting and harmful finetuning; further work is required to isolate the sources of these effects. These results suggest that RL to reinforce beneficial behavior in realistic domains can produce models that are more robustly aligned with human flourishing.

</details>


### [130] [Can Language Model Agents be Helpful Circuit Explainers in Mechanistic Interpretability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24026)
*Ayan Antik Khan,Harsh Kohli,Yuekun Yao,Huan Sun,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyVE方法，利用语言模型代理自动解释已定位的神经网络回路，并通过AgenticInterpBench基准测试验证其有效性，发现强模型能生成合理假设，但可靠验证仍是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性在自动定位回路方面取得了进展，但解释这些已定位组件的功能仍然依赖人工劳动，耗时且难以标准化。因此，研究者希望探索语言模型代理能否辅助解决这一解释问题。

Method: 首先构建了包含84个半合成Transformer回路和163个组件级标注的基准测试AgenticInterpBench。然后提出HyVE（假设、验证、解释）代理式解释器，通过观察、假设生成、因果验证的迭代循环，为每个组件生成解释和回路级任务描述。在四种不同语言模型主干上进行了实验评估。

Result: HyVE在四种语言模型主干上都能恢复有用的组件级和任务级解释，但没有一个主干全面最优。强骨干模型通常能形成基于观测的合理假设，失败更多发生在验证循环后期，原因包括不完整的验证计划、代码执行错误或未解决的假设。在Llama-3-8B算术回路的案例研究中，该方法能扩展到自然训练的模型。

Conclusion: 语言模型代理作为回路解释器具有潜力，但可靠的验证仍然是最关键的障碍。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has made substantial progress in automatically localizing circuits, but explaining what localized components do remains labor-intensive and difficult to standardize. In this work, we study whether language model (LM) agents can assist with this explanation problem once a circuit has already been identified. We introduce AgenticInterpBench, a benchmark for circuit explanation built from 84 semi-synthetic transformer circuits with 163 component-level annotations. We propose HyVE (Hypothesize, Validate, Explain), an agentic explainer that analyzes each component through an iterative loop of observation, hypothesis generation, and causal validation, eventually producing a component-level explanation and a circuit-level task description. Across four LM backbones, HyVE recovers useful component- and task-level explanations, but no backbone is uniformly best. Our analysis shows that strong backbones usually form observation-grounded hypotheses, while failures more often arise later in the validation loop, through incomplete validation plans, code execution errors, or unresolved hypotheses. A case study on an arithmetic circuit in Llama-3-8B shows that the same formulation can extend beyond semi-synthetic benchmarks to naturally trained models. Overall, LM agents are promising circuit explainers, but reliable validation remains the key obstacle.

</details>


### [131] [Breaking the Filter Bubble: A Semantic Pareto-DQN Framework for Multi-Objective Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24042)
*Cláudio Lúcio Do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva,André de Oliveira Brandão*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多目标强化学习（Pareto-DQN）的推荐框架，通过将推荐形式化为语义多目标马尔可夫决策过程，在保持用户参与度的同时，有效提升了信息多样性和提供者公平性，打破了传统单目标推荐系统导致的过滤气泡和语义同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统单目标优化（如即时参与度）容易引发过滤气泡和语义同质化，且传统Deep Q-Networks等模型无法在平台留存与社会价值（信息多样性、公平性）之间进行有效权衡。

Method: 提出多目标强化学习框架，将推荐建模为语义多目标马尔可夫决策过程；结合高保真语义嵌入与Pareto-DQN智能体，将参与度、多样性和公平性作为独立、不可聚合的奖励信号；采用基于超体积的动作选择机制来避免静态奖励标量化的缺陷。

Result: 在MovieLens小规模数据集上的实验表明，基于超体积的动作选择能够打破语义崩溃的反馈循环，维持高状态轨迹方差，有效映射帕累托前沿，在仅对参与度产生边际影响的情况下实现了辅助社会目标的显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建内在对齐、负责任的推荐系统提供了可行路径，证明了多目标强化学习在平衡商业目标与社会价值方面的潜力。

Abstract: Recommender systems often induce filter bubbles and semantic homogenization by monolithically optimizing for immediate user engagement. Standard single-objective models, including traditional Deep Q-Networks, are ill-equipped to navigate the trade-offs between platform retention and critical societal values like information diversity and provider fairness. To address these limitations, we introduce a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework that formalizes recommendation as a semantic multi-objective Markov decision process. By integrating high-fidelity semantic embeddings with a Pareto-DQN agent, our architecture treats engagement, diversity, and fairness as distinct, non-aggregable reward signals, avoiding the pitfalls of static reward scalarization. Empirical evaluations on the MovieLens small dataset shows that our hypervolume based action selection disrupts the feedback loops responsible for semantic collapse. By sustaining high state-trajectory variance, the Pareto-DQN effectively maps the Pareto frontier, achieving gains in auxiliary societal objectives with only marginal impacts on engagement. This work provides a path toward intrinsically aligned, responsible recommender systems.

</details>


### [132] [Ensemble Feature Selection and Harris Hawks Optimization for Explainable Mental Health Risk Prediction in Female Sex Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24047)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Md. Parvej Hoque Palash,Shahriar Siddique Ayon,Ramkrishna Saha,Abdullah Al Mamun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合预测模型，结合ANOVA与互信息的集成特征选择策略和哈里斯鹰优化调参的逻辑回归，用于预测女性性工作者的抑郁风险。模型在3005名FSWs上取得95.78%准确率、95.77% F1分数和0.96 AUC，识别出创伤后应激、客户暴力和职业因素为抑郁主要贡献因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性性工作者面临暴力、污名化和经济困难，抑郁等心理障碍风险高。现有机器学习模型难以捕捉这一边缘群体中存在的高维复杂风险模式，亟需更有效的预测工具。

Method: 采用集成特征选择策略（ANOVA+互信息）提取关键特征，结合哈里斯鹰优化算法调参逻辑回归模型，并引入可解释AI方法分析模型预测背后的创伤相关因素。

Result: 模型在3005名FSWs上达到95.78%准确率、95.77% F1分数和0.96 AUC，优于传统分类器；识别出创伤后应激、客户暴力和职业因素为抑郁的主要预测因子。

Conclusion: 该混合模型弥合了传统方法与机器学习之间的差距，开发出可解释的AI工具，能够为弱势群体提供早期干预、循证心理社会关怀和健康规划支持。

Abstract: One of the significant mental health issues affecting female sex workers (FSWs) is mental disorders, especially depression. Exposure to violence, stigma, and economic hardship further increases their psychological risk. Current machine learning (ML) models are typically ineffective at capturing the high-dimensional and complex risk patterns that exist in this marginalized group. This paper suggests a hybrid predictive model that merges an ensemble feature selection strategy using ANOVA and mutual information and Harris Hawks optimization-tuned logistic regression and represents a new application of swarm intelligence to predict mental health in vulnerable groups. The explainable AI (XAI) methods can be used to understand the factors of trauma associated with model predictions. When applied to a group of 3,005 FSWs, it can be seen that the proposed model is more effective than traditional classifiers, with an accuracy of 95.78%, an F1 score of 95.77%, and an AUC of 0.96, and identifying post-traumatic stress, client-related violence, and occupational factors as major contributors to depression. This work bridges the gaps between conventional and ML approaches to develop an XAI tool that enables vulnerable groups to receive early assistance, evidence-based targeted psychosocial care, and health planning.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Trajectory Imitation: Strategy-Guided Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24064)
*Tianyuan Shi,Canbin Huang,Bei Li,Xin Chen,Xiaojun Quan,Jingang Wang,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出策略引导策略优化（SGPO），通过可复用的策略蒸馏替代实例级轨迹模仿，将强模型推理能力迁移至弱模型。在四个数学基准上，SGPO 持续优于 SFT、在线 RL 和混合策略基线，平均得分提升 2.2 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理能力蒸馏方法局限于模仿特定解题轨迹，仅迁移"回答什么"而非"如何推理"，导致模型记忆实例步骤而非习得可迁移的问题解决技能，难以泛化到新问题。

Method: SGPO 从强模型响应中提取结构化策略描述，为每个问题构建自主轨迹和策略引导轨迹。采用前向 KL 散度在 token 层级选择性迁移策略条件化带来的分布偏移，并配合近端约束确保稳定性；同时设计自适应实例级权重，在自主探索不足时增强引导，随模型能力提升而降低引导强度。

Result: 在四个数学基准上，SGPO 持续优于 SFT、在线 RL 和混合策略基线，在 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 上平均提升 2.2 分。前向 KL 目标提供选择性蒸馏信号，优于直接轨迹模仿；策略蒸馏与基础模型能力呈现互补缩放特性。

Conclusion: SGPO 通过策略级蒸馏替代轨迹级模仿，有效实现了"如何推理"的迁移，显著提升了弱模型的泛化推理能力。

Abstract: Distilling reasoning capabilities from strong to weak language models typically involves imitating specific solution trajectories, effectively transferring what to answer rather than how to reason. This trajectory-level imitation encourages memorization of instance-specific steps rather than acquisition of transferable problem-solving skills, limiting generalization to novel problems. We propose Strategy-Guided Policy Optimization (SGPO), which replaces instance-level trajectory imitation with reusable strategy distillation. SGPO extracts structured strategy descriptions from strong-model responses and, for each problem, constructs both autonomous and strategy-guided trajectories to enable direct comparison of the model's behavior with and without strategic guidance. The framework then addresses two key questions. For how to distill, a token-level forward-KL objective selectively transfers the distributional shift induced by strategy conditioning into the unguided policy, with proximal constraints ensuring stability. For when to distill, adaptive instance-level weighting strengthens guidance when autonomous exploration falls short and reduces it as the model's own competence grows. Experiments on four mathematical benchmarks across two model families show that SGPO consistently outperforms SFT, on-policy RL, and hybrid-policy baselines, improving the average score by 2.2 points over the strongest baseline on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. Analysis reveals that the forward-KL objective provides an inherently selective distillation signal that outperforms direct trajectory imitation, and that strategy distillation exhibits complementary scaling with base model capability.

</details>


### [134] [Exploring Academic Influence of Algorithms by Co-occurrence Network Based on Full-text of Academic Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24099)
*Yuzhuo Wang,Chengzhi Zhang,Min Song,Seong Deok Kim,Youngsoo Ko,Juhee Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于学术论文全文构建了NLP领域的大规模算法共现网络，从网络视角分析了算法的群体影响力及其随时间演化的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常孤立评估单个算法的影响力，忽视了算法之间通过相互连接形成的群体影响，缺乏从网络视角对算法集体影响力的系统分析。

Method: 利用深度学习模型从学术论文全文中提取算法实体，构建总体、累积和年度三种算法共现网络，采用多种中心性指标评估算法的群体影响力，并从时间维度分析网络演化特征。

Result: 算法网络呈现出典型复杂网络特征，近二十年来连接日益密集；经典、高性能以及处于不同研究时期交汇处的算法具有高流行度、控制力和中心性；算法影响力下降时，首先失去核心网络位置，随后与其他算法的关联减弱。

Conclusion: 这是首次对算法共现网络的大规模分析，覆盖四十余年的学术文献，提供了算法影响力的时间与结构视角，为未来研究算法、学者与任务之间的网络关系奠定了基础。

Abstract: Algorithms have become central to scientific research in the era of artificial intelligence (AI). Although algorithm mentions in papers are often used to indicate popularity and influence, existing studies usually evaluate individual algorithms in isolation and pay limited attention to the collective influence formed through their interconnections. This study constructs large-scale algorithm co-occurrence networks in natural language processing (NLP) based on the full text of academic papers and investigates algorithm influence from a network perspective. Using deep learning models, we extract algorithm entities and build overall, cumulative, and annual co-occurrence networks. We analyze their structural characteristics and apply multiple centrality measures to assess the group influence of algorithms across the whole field and over time. The results show that algorithm networks display typical features of complex networks, with increasingly dense connections developing over approximately two decades. Classic, high-performing algorithms and those located at the intersections of different research periods tend to have high popularity, control, centrality, and balanced influence. When the influence of an algorithm declines, it usually loses its core network position first, followed by weaker associations with other algorithms. This study is the first large-scale analysis of algorithm co-occurrence networks. Covering more than four decades of academic publications, it provides a temporal and structural view of algorithm influence and offers a foundation for future research on networks linking algorithms, scholars, and tasks.

</details>


### [135] [ReMMD: Realistic Multilingual Multi-Image Agentic Verification for Multimodal Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24112)
*Chenhao Dang,Dantong Zhu,Jun Yang,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReMMD，一个面向现实多语言多图像场景的多模态虚假信息检测框架，包含基准数据集ReMMDBench（500样本、2756张图像、五种语言）和验证智能体ReMMD-Agent（通过原子点分解和可复用证据集实现结构化检测），在五类真实性分类任务上达到41.80%准确率，并显著降低检测成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准和方法大多局限于短标题、单张图像、二元标签或单一操纵来源，无法应对现实社交帖子中多语言长文本、多张图像、混合来源和图文框架错误等复杂场景，且基于智能体的验证在真实证据搜索下成本高昂。

Method: ReMMD包含两个核心组件：ReMMDBench（500个真实样本，2756张图像，涵盖五种单语语言和两种跨语言设置，三维文本长度层级，多图像帖子，五类真实性标签，八类失真标签，证据来源及推理依据）和ReMMD-Agent（具有持久记忆的验证器，将帖子分解为原子点，构建可复用证据集，预测结构化的L1/L2/L3输出）。

Result: 在闭源系统、开源LVLMs、MMD-Agent和T2-Agent的对比中，ReMMD-Agent使用GPT-5.2在五类真实性分类上取得了最佳性能：准确率41.80%，宏F1值39.12%，同时比MMD-Agent降低17.5%成本，比T2-Agent降低79.9%成本。

Conclusion: ReMMD在更贴近现实的多语言多图像虚假信息检测场景下，实现了更好的性能与更低的成本，为多模态虚假信息检测提供了更全面的评估框架和更高效的方法。

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation detection is increasingly important because viral posts now combine long multilingual narratives, several images, mixed provenance, and subtle text--image framing errors. Existing benchmarks and methods remain poorly matched to this setting: they usually isolate short captions, single images, binary labels, or one manipulation source, while agentic verification remains costly under realistic evidence search. We present ReMMD, a realistic multilingual multi-image agentic verification framework for multimodal misinformation detection. ReMMD includes ReMMDBench, a real-world multimodal misinformation detection benchmark with 500 samples, 2,756 images, five monolingual languages, two cross-lingual settings, three text-length tiers, multi-image posts, five-way veracity labels, eight distortion labels, evidence provenance, and rationales. It also includes ReMMD-Agent, a persistent-memory verifier that decomposes posts into atomic points, builds a reusable evidence set, and predicts structured L1/L2/L3 outputs. Across proprietary systems, open LVLMs, MMD-Agent, and T2-Agent, ReMMD-Agent obtains the best five-way veracity performance, with 41.80% accuracy and 39.12% macro-F1 using GPT-5.2, while reducing cost by 17.5% relative to MMD-Agent and 79.9% relative to T2-Agent. The project is available at https://dang-ai.github.io/ReMMD.

</details>


### [136] [VeryTrace: Verifying Reasoning Traces through Compilable Formalism and Structured Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24124)
*Ninghan Zhong,Ahmet Ege Tanriverdi,Kaan Kale,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeryTrace是一种零样本验证与修复框架，将自然语言推理轨迹形式化为可编译的DSL表示，通过混合验证器实现逐步错误定位与修复，在数学、机器人规划和亲属关系推理三个领域显著提升了大模型的推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-Thought推理中的早期错误会悄无声息地传播，导致模型产生自信但错误的结论，现有方法缺乏对推理轨迹的结构化验证机制。

Method: 提出DSL将推理轨迹形式化，显式化步骤依赖关系、将定量内容转化为可执行表达式、通过演绎模式结构化语义推理；混合验证器结合确定性检查（计算正确性、依赖解析、约束满足）与LLM审计（非可机械化语义判断），实现逐级错误定位与修复。

Result: 在AIME 2025（竞赛数学）、LLM-BabyBench（机器人规划）和CLUTRR（亲属关系推理）三个领域，VeryTrace在零样本设置下显著优于基线，无需领域特定训练或上下文示例。

Conclusion: 形式化的轨迹验证能够在保持精度的同时实现跨领域泛化，验证了结构化推理表示对提升大模型推理可靠性的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-step reasoning with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting remains fragile: logical errors or hallucinations in early steps silently propagate, producing confident but incorrect conclusions. This paper presents VeryTrace, a zero-shot verification-and-repair framework that formalizes natural-language reasoning traces into a structured, compilable representation. VeryTrace introduces a Domain-Specific Language (DSL) that (i) makes step dependencies explicit, (ii) mechanizes quantitative content as executable expressions, and (iii) structures semantic inferences via deduction schemas. Our hybrid verifier combines deterministic checks for computational correctness, dependency resolution, and constraint satisfaction with targeted LLM audits for non-mechanizable semantic judgments, enabling step-level error localization and repair.
  Across three diverse domains-competition mathematics (AIME 2025), robotics planning (LLM-BabyBench), and kinship reasoning (CLUTRR), VeryTrace improves accuracy over zero-shot baselines on state-of-the-art LLMs without requiring domain-specific training or in-context examples, demonstrating that formalized trace verification achieves both precision and generalization.

</details>


### [137] [OmniPath: A Multi-Modal Agentic Framework for Auditing Wheelchair Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24129)
*ASM Mobarak Hossain,Nadim Mahmud,Vaskar Raychoudhury,Md Osman Gani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniPath是一个融合OSM网络拓扑与高密度航空LiDAR数据的系统，能以0.5米精度自动检测人行道上的坡度、横坡和垂直不连续等物理障碍，并按ADA标准评估严重程度，将静态地图转化为主动无障碍数据源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准地图（如OSM）虽然能记录路径位置，但无法传达轮椅使用者实际出行的物理感受（如坡度、路面不平），这种信息屏障严重影响了轮椅使用者的出行安全和可达性。

Method: 融合OSM网络拓扑与USGS 3DEP高密度航空LiDAR数据，构建高保真3D模型；通过智能体以0.5米增量虚拟遍历网络，量化运行坡度、横坡和垂直不连续等物理摩擦点；依据ADA合规标准计算加权严重度评分，将危害分为"Mild"到"Critical"等级；并在国家广场通过200个地面真实验证点进行分层随机抽样验证。

Result: 系统在高严重度危害检测上表现出可靠的诊断能力，Severe类别F1分数为0.60，Critical类别为0.58。

Conclusion: OmniPath通过自动化微观尺度检测，能够识别标准地图无法显示的"隐形"障碍，将静态数据集转化为可预测无障碍挑战的主动数据源，在用户出门前就预判潜在问题。

Abstract: For a wheelchair user, a standard blue line on a map is often a broken promise. While platforms like OpenStreetMap (OSM) successfully capture where a path is, they frequently fail to convey how it physically feels to travel on it. This information barrier is problematic for wheelchair users. To solve this issue, we present OmniPath, a system that moves from passive mapping to proactive environmental auditing. Our framework fuses the network topology of OSM with the submeter precision of high-density aerial LiDAR (USGS 3DEP) to create a high-fidelity 3D model of the pedestrian environment. Rather than simply routing a user, our agent virtually traverses the network, analyzing the surface in 0.5 meter increments. It rigorously quantifies physical friction points specifically running slope, cross slope, and vertical discontinuities against ADA compliance standards, calculating a weighted severity score to categorize hazards from ``Mild'' to ``Critical.'' To ensure real world reliability, we validated the system against 200 physical ground truth field surveys across the National Mall using stratified random sampling. The framework demonstrated strong diagnostic reliability for high-severity hazards, achieving F1-scores of 0.60 for Severe and 0.58 for critical categories. By automating this micro-scale inspection, OmniPath identifies the ``invisible'' barriers that standard maps miss, effectively transforming a static dataset into accessibility data source that anticipates accessibility challenges before the user ever leaves home.

</details>


### [138] [T2D-Bench: Evidence-Gated Evaluation of LLM Outputs for Type 2 Diabetes Using a Multi-Layer Clinical-Lifestyle Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24145)
*Saba A. Farahani,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: T2D-Bench 是一个基于知识图谱的评估框架，用于检测 LLM 在2型糖尿病建议中是否满足可验证的证据约束。测试发现 GPT-4o-mini 和 GPT-4o 分别有35%和33%的案例未能通过证据路径检查，而证据门控机制可识别并修正这些不合规输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 能生成临床流利的糖尿病建议，但常不遵守指南约束或缺乏对生活方式相关血糖声明的明确依据，亟需一个可复现的评估框架来量化并纠正这一问题。

Method: 构建多层临床-生活方式知识图谱（UMLS、DrugBank、SIDER、ADA标准），设计100个结构化场景（诊断、用药安全、生活方式冲突），使用证据门控检测未支持的遗漏并通过约束修订使输出达标。

Result: GPT-4o-mini 和 GPT-4o 分别有35%和33%的案例未能通过基准定义的证据路径检查。

Conclusion: 可计算的证据约束能够使 LLM 在糖尿病输出中的无依据临床遗漏变得明确、可测量且可修正。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can produce clinically fluent recommendations for type 2 diabetes while failing to satisfy guideline constraints or explicitly justify lifestyle-related glycemic claims. We present T2D-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evidence-gated evaluation framework for testing whether LLM outputs satisfy explicit, graph-checkable evidence requirements. T2D-Bench is built on a multi-layer clinical-lifestyle knowledge graph that combines a biomedical spine (UMLS, DrugBank, SIDER), computable ADA Standards of Care rules, and lifestyle knowledge connected through a mechanistic bridge to glycemic laboratory effects. Across 100 structured vignettes spanning diagnosis, medication safety, and adversarial lifestyle conflicts, baseline outputs failed benchmark-defined evidence-path checks in 35% of cases for GPT-4o-mini and 33% for GPT-4o. The evidence gate detects unsupported omissions and uses constrained revision to bring outputs into verifier-level compliance with benchmark-defined evidence requirements. These results show that computable evidence constraints can make unsupported clinical omissions explicit, measurable, and correctable in diabetes-focused LLM outputs.

</details>


### [139] [The Geometry Behind Diffusion and Flow Matching: Gradient Flows and Geodesics in Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24157)
*Yian Yao,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文统一了扩散模型与最优传输流匹配的理论框架：扩散模型对应Wasserstein流形上的自由能梯度流（JKO格式），流匹配对应测地线（Benamou-Brenier最小作用量原理），两者在同一黎曼流形上分别从初值问题和边值问题出发，到达相同终点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将扩散模型（DDPM、DDIM、NCSN/SMLD、Energy Matching）与最优传输流匹配（Flow Matching）置于统一的Wasserstein几何框架下，揭示其内在联系与区别，消除各方法之间的理论孤立感。

Method: 以二次Wasserstein流形为公共几何基底：扩散模型视为自由能F(ρ)=KL(ρ||π)的梯度流（Fokker-Planck方程）及其隐式欧拉离散（JKO格式）；流匹配视为Benamou-Brenier公式的最小作用量测地线。通过同一流形上的两种变分原理统一两类模型。

Result: 扩散模型（DDPM、DDIM、NCSN/SMLD、Energy Matching）本质是同一JKO方案的不同实现；流匹配对应确定性ODE沿直线路径；两者在相同流形上分别走梯度流路径和测地线路径，到达相同终点。

Conclusion: 扩散模型和最优传输流匹配是同一Wasserstein几何下的两种互补范式：扩散是初值问题的梯度流，流匹配是边值问题的测地线，两者殊途同归。

Abstract: The space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d$) of probability measures with finite second moment carries a natural geometry: the quadratic Wasserstein distance W_2 makes it a complete metric space and, following Otto, a (formal) Riemannian manifold whose geodesics are the optimal-transport interpolations. On this manifold, the gradient flow of the free energy F(rho) = KL(rho || π) is exactly the Fokker-Planck equation, and its implicit-Euler discretization is the JKO scheme. This is the geometry underlying diffusion models: the forward process descends the free energy, and each denoising step realizes one JKO step, which recovers DDPM, DDIM, NCSN/SMLD, and Energy Matching; this is one scheme, not separate theories. The same manifold supports a second variational principle. Its geodesics - the minimum-action curves of the Benamou-Brenier formula - are precisely the optimal-transport paths that Flow Matching learns. Fixing both endpoints and following the geodesic, generation becomes a deterministic ODE along a straight line, hence far fewer sampling steps. Placing both families of models on one manifold makes their relationship exact: diffusion follows a free-energy gradient flow, an initial-value problem; optimal-transport Flow Matching follows a Wasserstein geodesic, a boundary-value problem. The two reach the same endpoints along different paths.

</details>


### [140] [An Introduction to Causal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24160)
*Elias Bareinboim,Junzhe Zhang,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果推断与强化学习本质上是紧密相连的，通过将环境建模为结构因果模型，可以统一处理在线学习、离线学习和因果学习等不同学习范式，并引入了广义策略学习、模仿学习和反事实学习等新任务，形成了因果强化学习（CRL）这一新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断和强化学习虽然都基于反事实关系这一共同基础，但长期以来各自独立发展，缺乏交互。作者认为明确承认并数学化这种联系可以带来新的学习机会。

Method: 将强化学习环境分解为具有不同因果不变性的自主机制集合，建模为结构因果模型（SCM），从而统一处理在线学习、离线学习和因果演算学习等不同学习模式，并引入广义策略学习、模仿学习和反事实学习等新任务。

Result: 提出了因果强化学习（CRL）这一统一框架，揭示了多种学习范式之间的内在联系，并识别出现有文献中未被充分探索的几类学习环境。

Conclusion: 因果推断与强化学习的结合具有巨大潜力，因果强化学习为研究更广泛的策略学习问题提供了新视角和方法论基础。

Abstract: Causal inference provides a set of principles and tools that allow one to combine data and knowledge about an environment to reason with questions of counterfactual nature, i.e., what would have happened had reality been different, even when no data of this unrealized reality is currently available. Reinforcement learning provides methods to learn a policy that optimizes a specific measure (e.g., reward, regret) when the agent is deployed in an environment and pursues an exploratory, trial-and-error approach. These two disciplines have evolved independently and with virtually no interaction between them. We note that they operate over different aspects of the same building block, counterfactual relations, which makes them umbilically connected. Based on these observations, novel learning opportunities arise when this connection is explicitly acknowledged and mathematized. To realize this potential, we note that any environment where the RL agent is deployed can be decomposed as a collection of autonomous mechanisms with different causal invariances, parsimoniously modeled as a structural causal model; any standard RL setting implicitly encodes such a model. This formalization allows us to put under a unifying treatment different modes of learning, including online, off-policy, and causal calculus learning, which appear unrelated in the literature. However, these modalities are not exhaustive: we introduce several natural and pervasive classes of learning settings that entail novel dimensions of analysis. Specifically, we introduce and discuss through causal lenses generalized policy learning, where to intervene, imitation learning, and counterfactual learning. These tasks lead to a broader view of counterfactual learning and suggest great potential for studying causal inference and reinforcement learning side by side, which we call causal reinforcement learning (CRL).

</details>


### [141] [Data Scale, Not Latency, Shapes Cross-Lingual Encoder Transfer in Streaming ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24169)
*Nenad Banfic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多语言（ML）与纯英文（EN）编码器初始化在流式语音识别模型迁移至新语言时的表现差异，发现多语言初始化的优势仅体现在数据量有限时（100小时训练数据下WER降低约4.21个百分点），随着训练数据增加（至2500小时）优势消失（差距仅0.20个百分点）。这种优势与流式延迟无关，且4-bit量化对模型影响较小（WER增加约0.5个百分点）。结论：低数据量用多语言初始化，数据充足时两者无差异，延迟和量化策略可独立决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式语音识别模型适配新语言时，存在多语言编码器和纯英文编码器两种热启动方案。常见直觉认为多语言编码器在低数据量下更有优势，但这种优势能持续多久、是否受流式延迟放大、以及在量化部署后是否依然存在，这些问题尚未被系统回答。

Method: 使用0.6B参数的cache-aware FastConformer Transducer模型，在8种欧洲语言上，控制5个目标语言数据规模（100h到2500h）、3个流式延迟等级及离线解码、4个公开测试集进行系统对比实验。

Result: 多语言初始化的优势是数据受限的而非延迟受限的。在FLEURS测试集160ms延迟下，EN-ML的WER差距从100h的+4.21个百分点下降到2500h的+0.20个百分点（幂律衰减，数据量每翻倍优势约减半）。不同流式延迟等级下差距稳定，2500h时接近零。4-bit权重量化使编码器缩小约3倍，WER仅增加约0.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 低数据量时应使用多语言初始化，大数据量时两种初始化方式无实质差异，延迟和量化决策可独立于初始化选择进行。

Abstract: Adapting a streaming speech recognition model to a new language requires choosing between two plausible warm starts: a multilingual (ML) encoder or an English-only (EN) encoder. The common intuition is that the multilingual encoder should help most at low data, but it is unclear how long that advantage persists, whether tight streaming latency amplifies it, and whether it survives deployment quantization. We answer these questions with a controlled sweep of a 0.6 B-parameter cache-aware FastConformer transducer across eight European languages, up to five target-language data scales (100 h to 2500 h), three streaming tiers plus offline decoding, and up to four public test sets. The main result is that multilingual initialization is a data-limited advantage, not a latency-limited one. On FLEURS at 160 ms, the mean EN-ML word error rate (WER) gap falls from +4.21 percentage points (pp) at 100 h to +0.20 pp at 2500 h; a power-law fit summarizes this decay, with each doubling of target-language data roughly halving the remaining advantage. Across the three streaming tiers, the across-language mean EN-ML gap is approximately stable at each scale from 100 to 1000 h, and is near zero by 2500 h. Finally, 4-bit weight-only encoder quantization at the matched 560 ms streaming tier reduces the encoder footprint by about 3x, with an average FLEURS WER increase of about 0.5 pp. The resulting guideline is simple: use multilingual initialization in low-data regimes, treat the choice as effectively irrelevant at large data, and make latency and quantization decisions independently.

</details>


### [142] [Navigating User Behavior toward Personalized Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24196)
*Hengji Zhou,Yufeng Liu,Ye Liu,Yong Xu,Lianghao Xia,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NaviGen 提出了一种双标识符表示（协同编码+文本编码）结合两阶段 SFT+RL 训练的方法，实现从用户交互历史中学习生成个性化内容指令，在商品、游戏、短视频领域显著提升图像/视频生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AIGC 管线需要用户提供高质量创建指令，但用户通常难以详细描述视觉细节，导致生成结果与用户需求不匹配。因此需要一种方法从用户交互历史中自动生成可执行的个性化内容指令。

Method: 提出 NaviGen 框架：(1) 将每个物品表示为双标识符——协同编码（行为底物）+ 文本编码（语义桥梁），在单一 token 流中融合；(2) 两阶段 SFT+RL 流程：先通过演化搜索监督蒸馏偏好推理与指令写作能力，再通过层次化与自一致性奖励对齐生成与用户意图。

Result: 在商品、游戏、短视频三个领域实验中，NaviGen 在个性化图像/视频生成、下一物品预测、以及指令的具体性、相关性与可生成性方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: NaviGen 通过双标识符表示与两阶段训练范式，有效解决了从用户行为历史到可执行生成指令的映射问题，为个性化 AIGC 提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Modern AIGC pipelines deliver high-fidelity images and videos but presuppose a well-formed creation instruction, while end users rarely articulate visual details, leaving generators misaligned with user demand. We study personalized content generation, which turns a user's interaction history into an executable instruction for downstream synthesis, and identify two obstacles: behavior must be encoded in a form legible to language reasoning, and the model must acquire instruction-writing skill absent from both pretraining and behavior data. We propose NaviGen, which represents each item with a dual identifier coupling a collaborative code and a textual code as a behavioral substrate and a semantic bridge in one token stream. On this representation, a two-stage SFT+RL pipeline first distills preference reasoning and instruction writing from evolutionarily searched supervision, then aligns generation with user intent through hierarchical and self-consistent rewards. Experiments across product, game, and short-video domains show that NaviGen improves personalized image and video generation, strengthens next-item prediction, and yields more specific, relevant, and visually generatable instructions. Our code is anonymously released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/NaviGen.

</details>


### [143] [Exploring the relationship between human-centric AI and firm idiosyncratic risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24224)
*Zhen-Yuan Ralph Liu,Yu-Ting Wang,Jia-Jia Yan,Shivam Gupta,Mihalis Giannakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究工业5.0背景下，以人为中心的人工智能（HCAI）如何通过降低企业异质性风险（IR），并探讨数字化、运营效率、高管持股及CEO信息技术背景等因素的调节作用。基于2015-2023年中国上市公司面板数据，发现HCAI能显著降低企业IR，其中数字化和高管持股增强该效应，而运营效率和CEO信息技术背景则意外削弱之。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管工业5.0中HCAI的讨论广泛，但其对企业异质性风险（反映投资者对企业AI战略差异化反应的关键指标）的影响尚不明确，这对企业在技术革命中管理财务风险至关重要。

Method: 整合情境化AI理论与社会技术系统理论，将HCAI概念化为一种情境化AI战略。采用2015-2023年中国上市公司多源面板数据集进行实证分析。

Result: HCAI与更低的企业IR显著相关；数字化和高管持股强化了HCAI的风险降低效应；运营效率和CEO信息技术背景则意外地削弱了该效应。

Conclusion: 研究为AI时代的伦理AI治理和企业财务风险管理提供了理论贡献与实践启示。

Abstract: Despite the extensive discussions of human-centric AI (HCAI) in Industry 5.0, its effects on firms' idiosyncratic risks (IR) remains underexplored. This is an imperative issue for firms navigate financial risks during the current technological revolution, as IR reflects investor reactions to corporate heterogeneous AI strategies and implementations by isolating firm-level stock volatility from systematic factors. Integrating situated AI theory with social-technical systems theory, we conceptualise HCAI as a situated AI strategy that reduces AI-related ethical risks and fosters AI-Human synergies in firms' business operations, ultimately reducing IR by aligning with stakeholders' diverse expectations. Moreover, socio-technical factors, namely digitalisation, operational efficiency, executive shareholding, and CEOs with IT background, may moderate the HCAI-IR relationship. Using a multi-source panel dataset of Chinese listed firms from 2015 to 2023, we find that HCAI is associated with lower firm IR. Furthermore, digitalisation and executive shareholding strengthen this risk-reducing effect, whereas operational efficiency and CEOs with IT background surprisingly attenuate it. Our findings offer theoretical contributions and practical insights for both ethical AI governance and firm financial risk management in the AI era.

</details>


### [144] [FlowR2A: Learning Reward-to-Action Distribution for Multimodal Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24231)
*Xirui Li,Zhe Liu,Xiaoqing Ye,Wenhua Han,Yifeng Pan,Junyu Han,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowR2A 通过将仿真奖励从判别目标重构为生成条件，使用流匹配解码器学习奖励条件化的动作分布，统一了基于评分和基于锚点的规划方法，在 NAVSIM 基准上取得了 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态驾驶规划中基于评分方法（密集奖励但固定动作空间）与基于锚点方法（动态生成但稀疏监督）之间的长期矛盾。

Method: 提出 FlowR2A，将仿真奖励重构为生成条件，利用流匹配解码器从密集轨迹-奖励对中学习奖励条件化的动作分布，并引入细粒度每时间步奖励条件化和奖励噪声增强。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 和 v2 基准上取得 SOTA 结果，生成的多模态提案质量显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: FlowR2A 成功统一了两类规划范式的优势，通过生成式建模内化了动作与安全、进度、舒适度、规则合规性等结果之间的关联，并支持测试时可控采样。

Abstract: Multimodal driving planning faces a long-standing tension between two paradigms: scoring-based methods benefit from dense reward supervision but are confined to a fixed action vocabulary, while anchor-based methods generate proposals dynamically yet suffer from sparse supervision constrained to a single ground-truth trajectory. In this work, we propose FlowR2A, which resolves this tension by reframing simulation-based rewards from discriminative targets into generative conditions. By learning the reward-conditioned action distribution from dense trajectory-reward pairs with a flow-matching decoder, FlowR2A unifies the dense supervision of scoring-based methods with the proposal generation of anchor-based methods in a single generative model, forcing the model to internalize the correlation between an action and its outcomes in safety, progress, comfort, and rule compliance. To balance hard safety constraints against soft progress objectives, we introduce fine-grained per-timestep reward conditioning and reward noise augmentation. The generative formulation naturally supports controllable test-time sampling via reward guidance and anchored sampling, producing high-quality proposals. FlowR2A achieves state-of-the-art results on the NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks, with multimodal proposals of substantially higher quality than prior methods.

</details>


### [145] [SP-Mind: An Autonomous Reasoning Agent for Spatial Proteomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24235)
*Yucheng Yuan,Yuanfeng Ji,Zhongxiao Li,Ruijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SP-Mind 是首个自主 AI 智能体，能将自然语言查询转化为端到端的空间蛋白质组学分析流程，无需任务特定微调。研究同时提出了包含 102 个任务的综合基准 SP-Bench，在多项评估中达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空间蛋白质组学分析流程碎片化，依赖专家手动协调异构工具，限制了研究的可扩展性和可重复性。亟需一个统一、自动化的解决方案。

Method: 提出 SP-Mind 自主 AI 智能体，配备专家策划的生物分析技能和专用计算工具，将自然语言查询直接转换为端到端分析工作流。同时构建 SP-Bench 基准（102 个任务，18 个类别）用于评估。

Result: 在 SP-Bench 和已建立的下游任务上，SP-Mind 相较现有开源生物医学智能体基线取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SP-Mind 有效统一了空间蛋白质组学分析流程，提升了自动化水平和可重复性，为空间蛋白质组学分析提供了标准化、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Spatial proteomics enables single-cell-resolution characterization of protein expression within tissue architecture, playing a critical role in understanding tumor microenvironments and guiding precision medicine. However, current analysis workflows remain fragmented, requiring expert manual orchestration of heterogeneous tools and limiting research scalability and reproducibility. We present SP-Mind, the first autonomous AI agent designed to unify the spatial proteomics analysis pipeline, from raw multiplexed tissue imaging to downstream phenotype discovery. Equipped with expert-curated biological analysis skills and specialized computational tools, SP-Mind converts natural-language queries into end-to-end analytical workflows without task-specific fine-tuning. To rigorously evaluate its capabilities, we introduce SP-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning diverse tissue types, comprising 102 tasks across 18 distinct categories. Through extensive evaluation on SP-Bench and established downstream tasks, SP-Mind achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to existing open-source biomedical agent baselines.

</details>


### [146] [Towards Federated Long-Tailed Graph Learning: An Energy-Guided Dual Decoupling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24237)
*Lianshuai Guo,Zhongzheng Yuan,Xunkai Li,Meixia Qu,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FedEPD框架，通过拓扑净化与语义重校准的双重解耦范式，解决联邦图学习中的长尾分布问题，在多个基准上提升准确率最高4.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实联邦图学习中的数据类别常呈长尾分布，导致全局模型偏向多数类，且少数类节点被异构性主导的头部邻居结构隔离，现有方法在数据稀缺下易过拟合结构噪声。

Method: 采用双重解耦范式：(1) 基于分布感知的Dirichlet能量剪枝过滤空间异质边；(2) 从拓扑中心节点提取鲁棒全局原型，通过空间低通原型注入融入局部表示；(3) 两阶段交替优化保护多数类决策边界同时提升少数类精度。

Result: 在多种长尾基准上达到SOTA，准确率提升最高4.97%，Macro-F1提升最高5.48%。

Conclusion: FedEPD通过拓扑与语义的解耦处理，有效缓解了联邦图学习中长尾分布带来的性能退化问题。

Abstract: Federated Graph Learning facilitates collaborative graph modeling across distributed clients while preserving data privacy. However, real-world data categories frequently exhibit long-tailed distributions. Such statistical scarcity severely degrades performance in two ways: it biases the global model toward majority classes, and it structurally isolates minority nodes by submerging them in heterophilic, head-dominated neighborhoods. While existing methods attempt topology-agnostic statistical compensations, they often fail under data scarcity. Instead of recovering tail nodes, they overfit the structural noise from adjacent dominant classes, leading to representation degradation. To address these limitations, we propose FedEPD, a framework built on a dual decoupling paradigm that separates topological purification from semantic recalibration. Specifically, FedEPD utilizes distribution-aware Dirichlet energy pruning to filter spatial heterophilic edges. It then overcomes Non-IID distribution shifts by extracting robust global prototypes from topologically central nodes, which are incorporated into local representations via a spatial low-pass prototype injection. Furthermore, a two stage alternating optimization strategy strictly protects majority decision boundaries while improving minority accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedEPD achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse long-tailed benchmarks, yielding absolute improvements of up to 4.97% in Accuracy and 5.48% in Macro-F1.

</details>


### [147] [Probing the Misaligned Thinking Process of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24251)
*Kaiwen Zhou,Constantin Venhoff,Jonathan Michala,Xin Eric Wang,William Saunders*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过将不良行为分解为18种细粒度认知指标，利用线性探针从模型内部激活中检测这些指标，实现对大型语言模型不良行为的监控。实验表明，该方法在分布外基准上达到0.935 AUROC，且对良性对话误报率低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型表现出日益增长的策略性欺骗、消极应付、自我保护等不良行为，在高风险场景中可靠检测这些行为对确保安全负责的使用至关重要。

Method: 将不良行为分解为18种细粒度认知指标（不良行为指标）；通过自动化的元计划引导流程生成多轮训练对话；使用线性探针从模型内部激活中检测这些指标的存在。

Result: 在5种不良行为上，探针在分布外基准测试中达到0.935 AUROC（匹配强LLM评判），并在良性流量上保持较低的误报率。

Conclusion: 通过分解不良行为为可检测的认知指标并使用线性探针监控内部激活，是一种有效且可推广的不良行为检测方法，有助于确保LLM的安全可靠部署。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit a growing range of misaligned behaviors such as strategic deception, sandbagging, and self-preservation. As they are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, it is critical to reliably detect such behaviors to ensure safe and responsible use. In this work, we propose to monitor misalignment by decomposing it into fine-grained cognitive processes -- misalignment indicators -- and detecting their presence in a model's internal activations via linear probes. We develop a taxonomy of 18 indicators spanning different misaligned behaviors, paired with an automated, meta-plan-guided pipeline that generates multi-turn training conversations. To rigorously evaluate generalization, we construct an out-of-distribution suite combining automated behavioral elicitation, established misalignment benchmarks, and natural benign conversations. Across 5 misaligned behaviors, our probes match a strong LLM judge with 0.935 AUROC on out-of-distribution benchmarks while keeping a low false positive rate on benign traffic. We further perform in-depth analysis to understand the probes and the model's internal representations of misalignment indicators.

</details>


### [148] [Tractable Reasoning and Conjunctive Query Answering for Defeasible DL-Lite under Rational Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24279)
*Giovanni Casini,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了理性闭包（Rational Closure）在DL-Lite家族轻量级描述逻辑的核心和Horn变体中的应用，提出了一种基于现有经典推理器的插件架构，实现了在最小计算开销下高效的推理和 conjunctive query 回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非单调推理形式——理性闭包应用于轻量级描述逻辑DL-Lite，以处理可废止知识，同时保持高效性。

Method: 提出一种插件架构，基于现有的经典标准推理器，在DL-Lite中实现理性闭包下的实例检测和合取查询回答。

Result: 证明了在理性闭包下，DL-Lite的推理和合取查询回答可以在最小计算开销下高效完成。

Conclusion: 理性闭包可以有效地应用于DL-Lite系列逻辑，且通过插件架构能够复用现有推理器，实现高效的非单调推理。

Abstract: In Description Logics (DLs), reasoning under Rational Closure (RC) is a well-known and widely accepted non-monotonic formalism to handle defeasible knowledge. In this paper, we study the application of RC to the core and horn variants of the DL-Lite family of lightweight description logics. We analyze both entitlement (instance checking) and Conjunctive Query (CQ) answering under RC. Our main contribution is providing a plug-in architecture that builds upon existing standard classical reasoners, establishing that reasoning and CQ answering under RC for DL-Lite can be done efficiently with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [149] [LemonHarness Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24311)
*Kailong Ren,Fubo Sun,Jiachen Liu,Liu Yang,Zimo Yin,Jiaying Li,Congli Yin,Ming He,Yu Huo,Jiawei Liu,Zeping Chen,Yubin Huangfu,Ronghua Li,Yixuan Wu,Xing Su,Yanzhi Xu,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao,Lei Zhang,Xiaohui Geng,Jianping Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LemonHarness是一个面向长时程LLM Agent的集成执行框架，通过显式工作区边界、结构化工具接口、可复用规则知识库和时序感知执行机制，显著提升了Agent在复杂任务中的稳定性和准确性，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到84.49%-86.52%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
